Every live suite in `test_query/` picks up the module-scoped autouse fixture [`examples/fakeshop/test_query/conftest.py::_reload_project_schema_for_acceptance_tests`][fakeshop-test-library-reload] (see [`examples/fakeshop/test_query/README.md`][fakeshop-test-query-readme]), which rebuilds the full project schema through `examples/fakeshop/schema_reload.py::reload_all_project_schemas`; the filter binding re-runs on the post-reload `finalize_django_types()`.

## Doc updates

- [`docs/GLOSSARY.md`][glossary] — the [`FilterSet`][glossary-filterset], [`RelatedFilter`][glossary-relatedfilter], [`Meta.filterset_class`][glossary-metafilterset_class] and [`filter_input_type`][glossary-filter_input_type] entries carry the shipped contract: declarative `Meta.model` / `Meta.fields`; `RelatedFilter` cross-relation traversal; `check_*_permission` gates with active-input-only scope; the explicit-`queryset=` **scope boundary** (nested and flat filters through the declared branch cannot escape it; a path crossing no declaration is not bounded by it; not a visibility gate); flat relation leaves answering over the rows every declared hop they walk shows (like their nested twins, except `isNull: true` and `exclude=True`); logical-and / -or / -not; generated input types with stable class-derived names; cycle-safe lazy resolution (Layer 6 not ported). They sit under the Filtering category; the [Index][glossary-index] carries all four.
- [`docs/README.md`][docs-readme], [`README.md`][readme], [`TODAY.md`][today] list the filter surface among the shipped capabilities.
- [`docs/TREE.md`][tree] lists `filters/` in the current on-disk layout and the mirrored `tests/filters/` tree.
- [`GOAL.md`][goal] — the astronomy showcase references `Meta.filterset_class = filters.GalaxyFilter`; the migration narrative ([`GOAL.md #"migrates with a one-line parent-class swap"`][goal-migration-shape]) states the parent-class swap.

## Risks and open questions

- **Layer 5's forward-reference shape against Strawberry releases.** The idiom is `Annotated["{Name}FilterInputType", strawberry.lazy("django_strawberry_framework.filters.inputs")]` resolved via `LazyType.resolve_type` reading `module.__dict__`. The package's `strawberry-graphql` floor in [`pyproject.toml`][pyproject] supports it, and every schema the suite builds resolves these references (each generated input carries the self-referential `and` / `or` / `not` fields), so a Strawberry regression fails CI. If Strawberry ever drops the module-globals path, the namespace design is revisited.
- **`Annotated["NotYetMaterialized", strawberry.lazy(...)]` resolution timing.** The materialize-before-`Schema` ordering ([Decision 6](#decision-6--finalizer-phase-25-binding-seam--materialize-before-schema-ordering)) depends on consumers calling `finalize_django_types()` BEFORE `strawberry.Schema(...)`, the order [`docs/README.md`][docs-readme] pins. A consumer who constructs the schema first gets a `LazyType.resolve_type` failure naming the missing class, which surfaces the ordering bug at schema build.
- **Input-type namespace under per-`Meta.primary` secondary `DjangoType`s.** The namespace is name-keyed, so two `DjangoType`s on one model with two `filterset_class`es generate two distinct input types. No aliasing key exists for two filtersets that should share one input type.
- **`Meta.filterset_class` accepting a plain `django_filters.FilterSet`.** The validator rejects it; consumers migrate by parent-class swap ([`GOAL.md #"migrates with a one-line parent-class swap"`][goal-migration-shape]). No adapter for un-reparented `django-filter` classes exists.
- **Filter applied to a relation that has a custom `get_queryset`.** Step 3 of [Decision 8](#decision-8--relation-permission-cascade--get_queryset-cooperation) derives the child visibility queryset from the target type's `get_queryset` for every ACTIVE `RelatedFilter` branch BEFORE the parent's related constraint runs, so a nested filter cannot match a parent through a hidden child, and neither can a flat leaf walking the same declared branch. The optimizer's `Prefetch` downgrade ([Queryset diffing][glossary-queryset-diffing]) covers OUTPUT planning, which runs after the parent queryset is WHERE-filtered; it does not scope parent-row filtering.
- **Logical-and / logical-or input shape.** The cookbook's flat shape (`filter: { and: [...] }`) ports verbatim; `and` / `or` / `not` sit on every `<TypeName>FilterInputType` (including nested `RelatedFilter` branch inputs) but never inside a per-field lookup bag.
- **`Meta.fields = "__all__"` performance AND schema-size growth.** The BFS runs once at finalize and is cached, so per-request cost is unaffected. The produced input type's SDL grows with `field_count × lookup_count`; wide models inflate introspection responses and client codegen. The workaround is an explicit `Meta.fields` dict with narrower lookup sets.
- **`Meta.search_fields` planned** while `LOOKUP_PREFIXES` / `construct_search` already ship in `filters/inputs.py`, so the planned card consumes them without retrofit; no consumer surface reads them today.
- **Glossary entry parity.** `LazyRelatedClassMixin`, `FilterArgumentsFactory`, `FILTER_DEFAULTS`, and `LOOKUP_PREFIXES` are internal symbols not in [`docs/GLOSSARY.md`][glossary]; they are documented here and in module docstrings. A future card that surfaces one publicly lands its glossary entry.

## Out of scope (explicitly tracked elsewhere)

- **Ordering** ([`OrderSet`][glossary-orderset], [`Meta.orderset_class`][glossary-metaorderset_class], [`RelatedOrder`][glossary-relatedorder]) — [`DONE-028-0.0.8`][kanban]; reuses this subsystem's lazy-resolution architecture with `OrderSet` / `RelatedOrder` substituted, and carries the composition test.
- **Aggregation** ([`AggregateSet`][glossary-aggregateset], [`RelatedAggregate`][glossary-relatedaggregate], [`Meta.aggregate_class`][glossary-metaaggregate_class], [`get_child_queryset`][glossary-get_child_queryset]) — planned Layer-3 sidecar reusing Layers 1–4 of the pipeline.
- **Field selection** ([`FieldSet`][glossary-fieldset], [`Meta.fields_class`][glossary-metafields_class]) — planned; orthogonal to filtering (field selection gates result shape, filters gate result content).
- **Search fields** ([`Meta.search_fields`][glossary-metasearch_fields]) — planned; consumes `LOOKUP_PREFIXES` + `construct_search`.
- **Permissions cascade** ([`apply_cascade_permissions`][glossary-apply_cascade_permissions], [Per-field permission hooks][glossary-per-field-permission-hooks]) — their own cards; the cascade composes with the `check_*_permission` gates through `get_queryset` per [Decision 8](#decision-8--relation-permission-cascade--get_queryset-cooperation).
- **`DjangoConnectionField`** ([`DjangoConnectionField`][glossary-djangoconnectionfield]), **`DjangoNodeField`** ([`DjangoNodeField`][glossary-djangonodefield]), **`DjangoConnection`** ([`DjangoConnection`][glossary-djangoconnection]) and [Connection-aware optimizer planning][glossary-connection-aware-optimizer-planning] — their own specs; the connection field consumes this subsystem's factory output for its `filter:` argument.
- **[`DEFERRED_META_KEYS`][base] entries other than `"filterset_class"`** — `"aggregate_class"`, `"fields_class"`, `"search_fields"` ship under their own cards.

## Definition of done

1. [`docs/SPECS/spec-027-filters-0_0_8.md`][spec-027] (this document) is at the canonical structured filename per [Decision 1](#decision-1--spec-filename-and-canonical-naming), with companion [`docs/SPECS/appx/spec-027-filters-0_0_8-terms.csv`][spec-027-terms] anchoring every project-specific term to its [`docs/GLOSSARY.md`][glossary] heading; [`uv run python scripts/check_spec_glossary.py --spec docs/SPECS/spec-027-filters-0_0_8.md`][check-spec-glossary] passes.
2. [`django_strawberry_framework/filters/`][filters] is a subpackage with `__init__.py`, `base.py`, `sets.py`, `factories.py`, `inputs.py` per [Decision 2](#decision-2--subpackage-layout-and-public-export-surface); `__init__.py` re-exports `FilterSet`, `RelatedFilter`, `FilterInput`, `filter_input_type` and the primitives; the top-level package's `__all__` does not carry them.
3. `base.py` ships the parity-floor primitives per [Decision 4](#decision-4--upstream-primitives-parity-floor): `TypedFilter`, `ArrayFilter`, `ArrayFilterMethod`, `RangeFilter`, `RangeField`, `validate_range`, `ListFilter`, `ListFilterMethod`, `GlobalIDFilter`, `GlobalIDMultipleChoiceFilter`, the raw-key pair `RelationPkFilter` / `RelationPkMultipleFilter`, and `RelatedFilter`. `Filter` (`django_filters.Filter` itself) and `LazyRelatedClassMixin` (from [`django_strawberry_framework/sets_mixins.py`][sets-mixins]) are re-exports. Each value of a declared `RangeFilter` / `ListFilter` / `ArrayFilter` is cleaned as the column it compares (an `ArrayField`'s innermost element column at any nesting depth; each member of an `in` over one a whole array value, one that is no list `invalid_list`), a relation's as the target column it compares on (its `to_field`, else the primary key), never looked up as a row, one whose `lookup_expr` crosses a relation as the column the crossing reaches, its hops walked, scoped and gated as its `field_name="<relation>__<column>"` spelling's are, a `method=` filter's on a relation as sent, and one over a path only the queryset handed to the set resolves (an annotation, a `FilteredRelation` alias) as that queryset compares it (the Decision 4 rows). Two bounds of a `RangeFilter` compare as a range whatever lookup it declares (over an integer column, or a relation itself's compared column, as `gte` + `lte`), its declared lookup naming only the published key and its transforms staying on the compared path.
4. `sets.py` ships `FilterSetMetaclass` and `FilterSet` per Layers 3 + 4 of [Decision 3](#decision-3--six-layer-lazy-resolution-pipeline), a `RelatedFilter(flat=False)` declaration copying nothing and `MAX_FLAT_FILTERS` bounding net key growth (pinned by the files the [Test plan](#test-plan) names for them). `FilterSet` subclasses `django_filters.filterset.BaseFilterSet` per [Decision 5](#decision-5--django-filter-as-the-foundation) and carries: (a) the `FILTER_DEFAULTS` class attribute + `filter_for_field` / `filter_for_lookup` overrides applying the Relay-vs-scalar conditional per [Decision 4](#decision-4--upstream-primitives-parity-floor), so runtime filter instances and Strawberry input shape stay aligned, and marking a relation whose link onto its target reads non-pk columns to compile against `<relation>__pk`; (b) the `_owner_definition: DjangoTypeDefinition | None` slot bound at finalizer phase 2.5; (c) **the resolver-facing classmethod pair `apply_sync(input_value, queryset, info) -> QuerySet` and `async def apply_async(input_value, queryset, info) -> QuerySet`** running the [Decision 8](#decision-8--relation-permission-cascade--get_queryset-cooperation) pipeline (related visibility derivation, one per declared hop per request, shared by nested branches at every level, logical arms and flat leaves, normalization through the `filter_lookup_table` form keys, request resolution, active-branch related constraints applied before construction, active-input-only permission pass, the unstorable-text refusal, a widget's refusal of the value it reads and explicit `form.is_valid()` raising `GraphQLError("Invalid filter input", extensions={"code": "FILTER_INVALID", "errors": filterset.errors.get_json_data()})`, and `filterset.qs`, each flat leaf's cleaned value held to its integer field's range first, `_held_integer_value`); (c.1) the thin `apply(...)` dispatcher that runs `apply_sync(...)` inside `try / except SyncMisuseError` and re-raises a `RuntimeError` naming `apply_async`; (c.2) the named helpers (`_normalize_input`, `_derive_related_visibility_querysets_sync` / `_async`, `_run_permission_checks` — inherited from [`sets_mixins.py::ActiveInputPermissionMixin`][sets-mixins] — `_validate_form_or_raise`, `_apply_related_constraints`, `_branch_visibility_seed`, `_declared_hop_rows` / `_await_declared_hop_rows`, `_derive_flat_hop_visibility_async`); (d) `filter_queryset(self, queryset)` — the `django-filter` instance-method override for tree-form logic, distinct from `apply_sync` / `apply_async`; (e) the `get_fields` override pinning the whole-set sweep deltas of `Meta.fields = "__all__"` and of `Meta.exclude` with no `Meta.fields` (PK included unless `Meta.exclude` names it, M2M excluded), the per-field `"__all__"` expansion, and the refusals of a non-string entry, a malformed lookup-bag value, an unknown `Meta.exclude` name and a model-less sweep; (f) `Meta.model`, `Meta.fields`, `get_filters`, `check_permissions` on top of the upstream `declared_filters` / `base_filters` / `.filters` / `.form` / `.qs` / `Filter.method` machinery; (g) the `MAX_FLAT_FILTERS` limit on each set's expansion and the per-request copies of Layer 4 on the instances the package's own pipeline builds, any other construction copying every filter as django-filter does (`__init__` copies the set's own filters and the flat copies the data names; `filters` copies the rest when read; a bound form holds no flat copy the data does not name, and no unnamed copy is applied).
   For a sync flat annotation leaf, `django_strawberry_framework/filters/sets.py::FilterSet._prepare_annotation_hops_sync` reads the actual bound filter and final target after gates and before native cleaning only when a missing automatic list or range inner parser requires it. `django_strawberry_framework/filters/sets.py::FilterSet._postclean_binding_hops` reads complete fields after the entire actual form clean, and the request-wide visibility cache reuses the owner rows in the bind ask and application. Root and logical-arm forms in one apply input finish native cleaning before any new bind-only owner runs; an ordinary error suppresses those new hooks. `FilterSet._refresh_materialized_form_comparisons` preserves a consumer form already built in `__init__`. Ordinary sync flat leaves retain step 8; nested branches retain step 3; the async preawait remains as specified in Decision 8.
   Projected application uses the child-owned rows in `django_strawberry_framework/filters/sets.py::FilterSet._leaf_chain` and `django_strawberry_framework/filters/sets.py::FilterSet._apply_relation_leaf`, retaining the constructor restriction while the shared hook map holds the raw once-per-hop result. `django_strawberry_framework/filters/sets.py::FilterSet._refresh_materialized_form_comparisons` enriches only a missing inner comparison cleaner, preserving an existing filter- or form-owned inner field and its validators. `method=` list/range leaves remain on the ordinary sync flat step-8 path.
5. `factories.py` ships `FilterArgumentsFactory` (Layer 5 BFS on the shared `GeneratedInputArgumentsFactory`, deriving input field types from the resolved filter instances through `convert_filter_to_input_annotation`, NOT from a parallel `FILTER_DEFAULTS` lookup); it builds but does not materialize. Logical-operator fields use `list[Annotated["Name", strawberry.lazy(...)]] | None` for `and_` / `or_` and `Annotated["Name", strawberry.lazy(...)] | None` for `not_`.
6. `inputs.py` IS the input-class namespace per [Decision 9](#decision-9--input-class-namespace-vs-typeregistry-and-lifecycle): filter input classes are materialized as real module globals of `django_strawberry_framework.filters.inputs` via `materialize_input_class(name, cls)` (idempotent for `(name, input_class)` pairs by `is` identity; [`ConfigurationError`][glossary-configurationerror] on a name claimed by a different input class). The module ships `materialize_input_class`, `clear_filter_input_namespace`, `_materialized_names`, `_build_logic_fields`, `_build_input_fields`, `construct_search`, `LOOKUP_PREFIXES`, `LOOKUP_NAME_MAP`, `filter_lookup_table` (the per-class `FilterLookupTable` holding each operator-bag lookup's form key, read by `_build_input_fields` and `FilterSet._normalize_input`; a second filter on one `(head, lookup)` slot raises [`ConfigurationError`][glossary-configurationerror]), `convert_filter_to_input_annotation`, and `normalize_input_value(filter_instance, raw_value, field_name=None)`; `FieldSpec` and `build_input_class` are aliases for the shared generated-input substrate at [`django_strawberry_framework/utils/inputs.py`][utils-inputs]. A filter set's generated input name is [`sets_mixins.py::ClassBasedTypeNameMixin.type_name_for`][sets-mixins] (`<Name>InputType`), which the input builders call directly.
7. `filters/__init__.py` exports the helper in both spellings per [Decision 11](#decision-11--filter_input_typefilterset-consumer-helper): `FilterInput[BranchFilter]` / `filter_input_type(BranchFilter)` return `Annotated["BranchFilterInputType", strawberry.lazy("django_strawberry_framework.filters.inputs")]`, validate eagerly (`TypeError` for a non-`FilterSet`), and record the filterset into `_helper_referenced_filtersets` for the finalizer's orphan check.
8. [`django_strawberry_framework/types/definition.py::DjangoTypeDefinition`][definition] carries the `filterset_class` slot, populated from `Meta.filterset_class`, and `related_target_for(field_name)` — `(target_definition, model_field)` for relation fields, `None` otherwise — the per-segment lookup the GlobalID filter's request-time path walk consults.
9. [`django_strawberry_framework/types/base.py::DEFERRED_META_KEYS`][base] does not contain `"filterset_class"`; [`ALLOWED_META_KEYS`][base] does; `_validate_filterset_class` raises [`ConfigurationError`][glossary-configurationerror] for a non-`FilterSet`.
10. [`django_strawberry_framework/types/finalizer.py::finalize_django_types`][finalizer] runs the phase-2.5 filter-binding pass per [Decision 6](#decision-6--finalizer-phase-25-binding-seam--materialize-before-schema-ordering): bind every owner (a filterset declaring no `Meta.model` refused, model check + strict multi-owner check), expand every filterset, run `_audit_filterset_subpass_2_5`'s four audits (the first deep-copying every published table a wired filterset serves), orphan-validate `_helper_referenced_filtersets`, and only then materialize every built input class. The filter subsystem registers `clear_filter_input_namespace` (owner `filters.input_namespace`, `before_bind=True`) and the bound `_helper_referenced_filtersets.clear` (owner `filters.helper_references`) through `registry.py::register_subsystem_clear` at import time, and `registry.clear()` replays every registered callback.
11. `tests/filters/` carries the `__init__.py` shell, the `fixtures/` sub-package, the four source-mirroring modules (`test_base.py`, `test_sets.py`, `test_factories.py`, `test_inputs.py`), and `test_finalizer.py`, per the [Test plan](#test-plan).
12. [`tests/types/test_base.py`][test-types] and [`tests/types/test_definition_order.py`][test-types] carry the `Meta.filterset_class` promotion and cross-module tests; [`tests/types/test_definition_relations.py`][test-types] covers `DjangoTypeDefinition.related_target_for`.
13. [`examples/fakeshop/apps/library/`][fakeshop-library] ships `filters.py` (with `BranchFilter`, `ShelfFilter`, `BookFilter`, `LoanFilter`, `PatronFilter` and same-module `RelatedFilter("...")` references) AND `filters_genre.py` (`GenreFilter`, the cross-module fixture for the Layer-2 absolute-import-path branch).
14. [`examples/fakeshop/apps/library/schema.py`][fakeshop-library-schema] wires `Meta.filterset_class` on the corresponding `DjangoType` classes; root resolvers annotate `filter:` as `FilterInput[filters.<Name>Filter] | None` per [Decision 11](#decision-11--filter_input_typefilterset-consumer-helper) and call `apply_sync(filter, queryset, info)` AFTER `<OwnerType>.get_queryset(queryset, info)`.
15. [`examples/fakeshop/test_query/test_library_api.py`][fakeshop-test-library] carries the live filter contracts listed in the [Test plan](#test-plan); the Relay-vs-scalar pair pins [Decision 4](#decision-4--upstream-primitives-parity-floor)'s conditional end-to-end and the nested-visibility test and the flat-twin rows pin [Decision 8](#decision-8--relation-permission-cascade--get_queryset-cooperation)'s step 3 for both spellings, and [`examples/fakeshop/test_query/test_flat_copy_binding_api.py`][fakeshop-test-flat-copy-binding] pins a walked flat copy running as a filter of its hop's target set, model included.
16. [`docs/GLOSSARY.md`][glossary] carries the [`FilterSet`][glossary-filterset], [`RelatedFilter`][glossary-relatedfilter], [`Meta.filterset_class`][glossary-metafilterset_class] and [`filter_input_type`][glossary-filter_input_type] entries, under the Filtering category; the [Public exports][glossary-public-exports] section does not list them.
17. [`docs/TREE.md`][tree] lists `filters/` on disk and enumerates the mirrored `tests/filters/` tree.
18. Package coverage stays at 100% (`fail_under = 100` in `pyproject.toml [tool.coverage.report]`), gated by CI.
19. Slice 6 (the [`OrderSet`][glossary-orderset] composition test) lives at [`tests/orders/test_composition.py`][test-orders], carried by [`DONE-028-0.0.8`][kanban].
20. A declared choice filter publishes its column's enum only where its own form field accepts a member of it ([Decision 4](#decision-4--upstream-primitives-parity-floor), `ChoiceFilter` row); one accepting none is refused at build naming the column, pinned by the choice-refusal rows of the [Test plan](#test-plan). No deferred `choices` (a callable, a `lazy()` promise, a `SimpleLazyObject`, a choice iterator, a `QuerySet`) is evaluated at build under any choice form field, Django's own `TypedChoiceField` family and a CSV wrapper included, while each request's form evaluates them, pinned by the deferred-choices rows of the [Test plan](#test-plan).
21. A declared or `Meta.filter_overrides` leaf applied to its column with no transform whose own form and lookup answer no value of the column is refused at build naming the declaration, the column, the form field and the lookup (a CSV `in` / `range` wrapper asked as its member, a choice form on a column without `choices` asked its own keys, a form only shipped classes clean asked whatever its class, no query run), while one answering any value builds ([Decision 4](#decision-4--upstream-primitives-parity-floor), the catch-all row), pinned by the no-value refusal rows of the [Test plan](#test-plan).
22. A leaf of a set a connection serves (root, a nested `<relation>Connection`, a set reached through a `RelatedFilter`) whose path resolves to a lookup or transform class that is not Django's own is refused at build naming the set, the filter, the path, the lookup and the class, while a Django class under any lookup name, a `method=` filter, a `filter` override, an annotation path and a set no connection serves build ([Decision 6](#decision-6--finalizer-phase-25-binding-seam--materialize-before-schema-ordering)); `"__all__"` publishes no such class on any set ([Decision 3](#decision-3--six-layer-lazy-resolution-pipeline)).
23. A declared leaf running a shipped `filter` whose `field_name` and `lookup_expr` Django resolves on no queryset (a path ending on a column or a relation, to-one or to-many, whose walk refuses the leftover, the relation asked through its last join) is refused when its set publishes, at build for a served set and at first use for an unbound one, naming the set, the filter, the path, the lookup, the refused part and the field (a relation's with its related column spelled past it), a raw set declaring a refused to-many key leaf refused at its first construction walked or not, while a `method=` filter, a `filter` override, an annotation or alias path, a name past a relation and a range applied as a relation's bounds build ([Decision 4](#decision-4--upstream-primitives-parity-floor), `django_strawberry_framework/filters/base.py::refuse_unresolvable_lookup`), pinned by the unresolvable-predicate rows of the [Test plan](#test-plan). A published `ListFilter` / `ArrayFilter` whose Django lookup compares one value is refused at the same times, naming the set, the filter, the path, the lookup and the Django lookup it applies (a relation's related column spelled past it), while `in`, `range`, a transform ending in either, `has_keys` / `has_any_keys`, a `JSONField`'s, `RangeField`'s or `CompositePrimaryKey`'s own comparisons, an `ArrayField` column's value, a `method=` filter, a project lookup, a project or third-party field class and a stock django-filter parent build, and a `range` list of other than two members is the form's `invalid_values` error (`django_strawberry_framework/filters/base.py::refuse_listless_lookup`, `django_strawberry_framework/filters/base.py::_ElementField.validate`), pinned by the list-filter lookup rows of the [Test plan](#test-plan).
24. A class-level `filter` override on a relation key a flat leaf walks (to-many at any hop count, forward at two or more hops) whose value names rows of the relation's target runs once as declared, with its own `field_name`, the value its form cleaned and its own set's rows, and keeps only the parents reaching through visible hop rows a visible last-hop row its value names, its value naming the rows it keeps (`exclude=True` negates); a value naming no row of the target (one the shipped comparison skips or refuses included) runs on a to-many key as declared over the set's own rows with its relation (a many-to-many's through rows included, a first non-null forward key the database enforces joined round its target) joined only to the visible related rows and the walked lookup, a default, a path past the relation and Django's own refusal answering as unwalked, a relation its own Python names reading every row, a many-to-many whose through key onto its source Django gives no reverse name of its own (an auto-created self-referential one read forward, symmetrical or not) keeping the leaf re-bound to the target key over the visible targets, and on a forward key compares the stored key it holds; an `isnull` override runs over the visible related rows ([Decision 8](#decision-8--relation-permission-cascade--get_queryset-cooperation) step 3, `django_strawberry_framework/filters/sets.py::_KeyMatch`, `django_strawberry_framework/filters/sets.py::FilterSet._request_chain`, `django_strawberry_framework/filters/sets.py::FilterSet._relation_view`), pinned by `examples/fakeshop/test_query/test_relation_key_overrides_api.py`.
25. Raw form data under the package's `FilterSet` (`is_valid()` / `errors` / `.qs`, `DjangoFilterBackend`, `FilterView`) answers a value the typed input refuses (the unstorable-text refusal, a value the queryset's database cannot bind) as a form error with the typed input's message and code, never the driver's error: `DjangoFilterBackend`'s 400, an empty `strict` `FilterView`, a bare `.qs` applying the leaves that cleaned; a form error and a bind refusal are reported together; a consumer `form` override keeps its own raw form, while the apply pipeline runs text preflight, its one native clean, input-wide suppression, postclean owner acquisition and one held bind ask before `_validate_form_or_raise` reads those same form errors ([Edge cases](#edge-cases-and-constraints)). A generated form reused as a later set's `Meta.form`, including through consumer subclasses and repeated reuse, runs one current-owner text preflight before the entire consumer clean and one bind ask after it; inherited generated asks delegate to that owner. An empty-permitted unchanged form delegates to Django without asks when its outer consumer clean adds no value; an active post-super value joins the same postclean bind phase. A known refusal from its actual normalized widget during change detection remains a field error. A flat leaf walking a related target annotation, whether through a declared or a scoped undeclared hop, is asked against its rebound terminal filter and visible target queryset before eligibility is decided; projected child copies use the same actual target context. Raw tuple membership values are checked for unstorable text at every nesting depth before element cleaning. A handed `ExtractYear` / `ExtractIsoYear` alias whose shipped exact/gte or package range emits native `YearLookup` bounds returns this form-owned refusal for an impossible year; raw `.qs` then keeps a valid sibling after removing only the refused leaf. An `ExtractMonth` alias `in` member beyond the database integer range is refused for the whole package `ListFilter` or `ArrayFilter` field, even beside a fitting member; a registered integer `Abs` transform follows the same bind ask through its lookup-walk node types. A scoped undeclared `ExtractYear` bound and `ExtractMonth` member use the visible child Query for that refusal; scoped `F` and `Count` aliases and a stored generated integer column retain ordinary integer member dropping and two-bound widening, while native CSV numeric range keeps its own predicate. Complete annotation fields wait for the entire actual consumer form clean, including `Form.clean` and post-super code; one root/logical validation domain native-cleans all forms before any new bind-only owner, and a native error on any form suppresses it. A direct raw logical `.qs` reuses those same staged forms, leaves a suppressed uncached annotation leaf unapplied, and retains safe partial siblings.
26. A declared leaf typed from a field no `SCALAR_MAP` entry covers (a relation, a `GenericForeignKey`, a `CompositePrimaryKey`, an unregistered column, a related key column) is refused when the schema is built (an unserved set's at the `apply_sync` request sending an object to it) naming the filter as declared and the remedy its field takes, each remedy building ([Edge cases](#edge-cases-and-constraints)).
27. Over an `ArrayField` column a list filter types only `exact`, `contains`, `contained_by` and `overlap`, and a `BooleanFilter` the null test: a served set's per-field `"__all__"` under a `Meta.filter_overrides` list filter is refused at build naming the first lookup it cannot type, listing the four builds, and a list, CSV, typed or choice filter testing `isnull` is refused naming `BooleanFilter(lookup_expr="isnull")` ([Decision 3](#decision-3--six-layer-lazy-resolution-pipeline), [Decision 4](#decision-4--upstream-primitives-parity-floor), the Edge case and its Test plan rows).
28. A set built outside the apply pipeline with a request (`DjangoFilterBackend`, `FilterView`, a consumer's own construction) hands every `get_queryset` its walks, hops and logical arms reach an `info` whose `context.request` is that request ([Decision 8](#direct-construction-actor)), so each answer equals the same filter's over GraphQL for the same user; with no request each hook receives `None`.
29. A set's first construction publishes its filter table (`FilterSet.__init__`, [Decision 8](#direct-construction-actor)): a set no type serves and a plain subclass of a served set, built by a consumer, `DjangoFilterBackend` or `FilterView`, answer a walked key with the served set's rows, a declaration the publication refuses raises `ConfigurationError` at every construction, and a construction while the set cannot publish copies the table its re-entering read answers.
30. A `RelatedFilter` head raw form data names, own or copied, walks its path as a relation key and refuses a hidden row's key as a missing one's, its null value testing the rows each relation shows and each declaration's `queryset=` admits; a head the form leaves out, a `method=` head and one crossing a `RelatedFilter` whose target model no `DjangoType` is registered for run as declared, and a head overriding `filter` follows the overridden relation-key rule; a walk or a nested branch through a sliced `queryset=` answers the visible rows the slice holds ([Decision 8](#raw-form-heads)).
31. A declared branch's explicit `queryset=` admits the child rows among the rows it holds: a child filter django-filter runs `distinct()` for, a target `get_queryset` returning `distinct()` rows and a sliced, `distinct()` or combined `queryset=` answer the visible rows the queryset holds, nested, flat, under `not` / `and` / `or` and through raw form data; a `queryset=` keyed on another model is refused with `ConfigurationError` when its branch is active, and one pinned by `.using` to an alias the request does not read is refused ([Decision 8](#decision-8--relation-permission-cascade--get_queryset-cooperation) step 4a). It bounds only the paths whose walk crosses the declaration: a dotted key whose walk crosses no declaration of it (another set's, declaring no such branch) answers for its hops' target types alone ([Decision 8](#decision-8--relation-permission-cascade--get_queryset-cooperation) step 3).
